================================================================================
Borkeo EMR Update Note: Version 42.6.0
Midwife maternal services and the official ANC record
================================================================================

Date: 2026-10-23
Previous Version: 42.5.0
New Version: 42.6.0
Patient data: not touched. The new record type (ANC pregnancy record) stores
additively; old ANC forms open read-only with a link to the new record. Database
tables: new clinical_forms maternal service records, anc_visit_tasks. Additive
columns: risk_scores.color_history. Nothing removed.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. WHAT WAS WRONG
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
a) ANC, PNC, family planning and cervical screening were OPD doctor consultations;
   the midwife did not have her own maternal services queue. The vaccination
   service was missing. Safe abortion was in OPD instead of the Gynecology ward.
b) The ANC form (#sub_anc) did not match the official 2026 ANC record pages 2-5;
   it had no colour code, no visit schedule, no auto-fill from Triage, no GA
   from the LMP, no link between the rapid-test results and the lab table, and
   it created one record per visit instead of one per pregnancy.
c) The midwife had no inbox and no way to send a cervical result to a doctor.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2. WHAT CHANGED
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
a) Routing: ANC (anc), PNC (pnc), family planning (family-planning) and cervical
   screening (cervix-screening) go Registration -> Triage -> Cashier -> midwife
   queue. Vaccination (vaccination) goes Triage -> midwife directly (free, no
   Cashier). Safe abortion (safe-abortion) goes to the Gynecology ward queue
   instead of the OPD doctor; the old safe abortion forms stay readable. Reports
   count all of them as OPD services.
b) New page "បញ្ជីអ្នកជំងឺសេវាមាតា" (maternal services queue, /maternal-queue) for
   MIDWIFE of the Obstetric ward (public/nav-config.js group care, server/
   access.mjs roleCanPage): today's paid ANC / PNC / family planning / cervical
   screening patients and vaccination patients, with a call button like the OPD
   queue. Opening a patient opens the right form. Real midwife accounts and the
   Superuser switched to Midwife both see it.
c) Official ANC record (/anc-record, protected/anc-record.html, public/
   anc-record.js) one per pregnancy, linked to the patient's Health ID, opened
   at the first ANC visit and reused at every visit until delivery, referral to
   CPA3 or closing. The old ANC form opens read-only with a link to the new
   record. Saved values never move: the old records stay exactly as they were.

   Pages 2-5 of the 2026 official record (same order, same Khmer labels, same
   tables): identity, LMP, EDD (LMP + 280 days), EDD by first-trimester
   ultrasound, height, weight and BMI before pregnancy; general, surgical, drug
   allergy (linked to the EMR's structured allergy record), family,
   gynaecological and social history; risk factors; mouth and teeth check;
   anaemia check; previous pregnancies table with the 10 complications of the
   page-2 footnote; lab results table by date (HIV, Syphilis, HBsAg, Anti HCV,
   Hb, HCT, PLT, Group/RH, 3 free rows); ultrasound table (DATE, GA, presenta-
   tion, BPD, placenta, AFI, weight, EDD, other); visit table (DATE, GA, UH,
   FP, FHR, BP&P, Weight, WP, Proteinuria, Glucosuria, Varices, Fer, Vaccina-
   tion, Chief complaint, Diagnosis and intervention, Appointment, NAME).

   Khmer labels from server/catalogues/anc-record-labels.json (transcribed
   exactly from the page images; uncertain words are listed in docs/
   anc-khmer-to-confirm.md for the owner to check against the paper record).

   Print pages 2-5 in the official A4 landscape layout with the colour box
   ticked (public/anc-record.css print rules).
d) Auto-fill (always editable by the midwife; the original values are saved and
   never overwritten): name, date of birth and age from Registration; weight,
   height, BP, pulse, temperature and SpO2 from the Triage of the same visit;
   BMI, weight gain, GA and EDD calculated; midwife name from the login; tetanus
   vaccination from the vaccination page (TT1 / TT2 appears in the visit table
   when the patient is pregnant). Released Laboratory results fill the lab table
   and released Imaging reports the ultrasound table; the midwife's own rapid
   tests (HIV, syphilis, Hb) are typed in the visit form and also appear in the
   lab table.
e) GA and EDD: from the LMP (EDD = LMP + 280 days). A dating ultrasound before
   14 weeks whose EDD differs by more than 7 days from the LMP EDD is used
   instead; the source is always shown (public/anc-engine.mjs).
f) Colour code from server/catalogues/anc-color-chart.json (copied unchanged
   from borkeo-update-files/anc/, 69 items in 4 sections): the "auto" items
   decide from the data (age, height, BMI, BP, previous pregnancies, Hb,
   syphilis, HIV, twins), the others are tick boxes by the midwife. The highest
   level wins (red > yellow > green); the result is re-checked at every visit
   and every save. The official boxes បៃតង / លឿង / ក្រហម show at the top of the
   record and as a badge in the patient banner on every page; every colour
   change is kept with its date, reasons and the midwife who saved it.
g) Visit schedule from server/catalogues/anc-visit-schedule.json (copied
   unchanged from borkeo-update-files/anc/, green 4 visits page 7, yellow and
   red 8 visits page 8): which visit this is by GA and colour, its tasks
   (records, lab, exam, ultrasound, care) as check items, overdue tasks from
   earlier visits in red, and "មិនមែនជាការពិនិត្យតាមកាលវិភាគ" / "Not a scheduled
   visit" between the visits. The to-do list is a side panel always visible and
   also opens once when the record opens.

   A task acts: the midwife's own tests (HIV, syphilis, Hb, urine) open the
   result field; HBsAg / OGTT / CBC / ultrasound create the paraclinic order
   (patient pays at the Cashier and the order goes to the Laboratory or
   Imaging); iron and folic acid add the prescription; tetanus vaccine opens
   the vaccination entry (free, direct, the dose also appears in the visit
   table). A task can be closed undone only with a reason: មិនមានតេស្ត /
   អ្នកជំងឺបដិសេធ / ធ្វើរួចនៅកន្លែងផ្សេង / ផ្សេងៗ (text).

   Next appointment: suggested from the date of the next scheduled visit for
   the colour; the midwife can change it.
h) Yellow shows "ANC continues at Borkeo Referral Hospital (CPA2), 8 visits".
   Red shows the referral button: a DOCTOR or CHIEF_WARD of Obstetric or
   Gynecology signs it with the existing e-signature; the referral letter prints
   (public/anc-referral-letter.mjs: letterhead, patient, GA, colour and red
   reasons, vital signs, test results, treatment given, referring doctor
   signature line); the visit is marked "referred to CPA3" for reports. The
   layout is kept easy to replace by the official form later (the owner will
   upload it).
i) Midwife permissions: she opens and saves the forms of these patients
   (server/encounter-access.mjs: canOpenRecord, canSaveRecord by service code);
   orders HBsAg from the Laboratory and ultrasound from Imaging and nothing else
   (server/routes/paraclinic.mjs: MIDWIFE allowed for HBsAg and ultrasound
   orders only); prescribes only ferrous salt, ferrous salt + folic acid, folic
   acid, mebendazole, calcium and the vaccines of the vaccination service (the
   medicine search is filtered by role and service for MIDWIFE in public/
   medicine-autocomplete.js and checked on the server in server/
   pharmacy-prescriptions.mjs). The list of allowed medicines is in
   server/clinical-options.json midwifeAllowedMedicines and is easy to change.
j) Vaccination page (/vaccination, protected/vaccination.html, public/
   vaccination.js) for all ages, free: the vaccine is chosen from the medicine
   list lines marked vaccine (no filter; the national schedule vaccines are in
   the list), with dose number, date, batch number, injection site, next
   appointment and reaction. Each dose is deducted from stock (server/
   maternal.mjs: same stock movement as a dispensed medicine, reference = the
   vaccination id); the same vaccine and dose number twice gives a warning.
   Tetanus given to a pregnant woman also appears in the visit table of her ANC
   record (the TT1 and TT2 dose numbers). Vaccination history per Health ID with
   the next appointment; a vaccination card section prints.
k) Cervical screening: the midwife saves the result in the OPD consultation
   (server/routes/patients.mjs checks the role, no other role can use this
   endpoint) and it is sent as a staff message to the Gynecology ward doctors'
   inbox (existing staff messages) with the patient link; the doctor's
   acknowledgement completes the message. The send button no longer needs the
   required fields of the whole OPD consultation to be filled first: it saves
   nothing into the record (data-required-bypass in public/maternal-emr.js), it
   checks its own result field, and the server checks the role before accepting
   the save.
l) Safe abortion is billed under the Gynecology ward (server/finance.mjs
   departmentForServiceCode: safe-abortion -> gynecology). Old safe abortion
   records stay readable.
m) Defaults the owner may change later are kept together in server/
   clinical-options.json (chart gaps for age 18-19 and 35, height under 145 cm,
   BMI under 18.5, BP, number of births, twins as red; the visit window of
   +/- 1 week for the single-week visits 26, 36, 38 and 40; the paid and free
   service codes; the midwife's medicines).
n) Khmer: the record, colour chart and visit schedule text is copied from
   borkeo-update-files/anc/ and never retyped from memory; the words that could
   not be read with certainty on the page images are listed for the owner in
   docs/anc-khmer-to-confirm.md. Other new texts are in docs/
   translations-needed.csv marked "draft Khmer, owner to review".

Checked in a test copy with synthetic patients only, in Microsoft Edge
(tests/v426-midwife-anc-browser.mjs): a green pregnancy (4 visits; HBsAg and
ultrasound orders; iron prescription; task closed with a reason; tetanus in the
vaccination page and in the visit table), a yellow pregnancy (40-year-old, BMI
17.5, OGTT task at 26 weeks, next appointment, CPA2 8-visit message), a red
pregnancy (Hb 6.0, twins ticked, BP 165/112, previous two caesareans; doctor-
signed referral letter). A cervical screening message sent to the Gynecology
doctors, a vaccination deducting stock (warning when the same dose was typed
twice), a safe abortion patient in the Gynecology ward queue. The old ANC form
opens read-only with "កំណត់ចំណាំ៖ Open the new ANC record" and the link. Real
staff menus are otherwise unchanged.
npm test: 946 of 946 pass. npm run check:khmer passes.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
4. INSTALLATION (one step at a time)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The files are already updated in this folder. If you use this folder as is,
only steps 1 to 4 are needed.

Step 1: Close the EMR server window (or press Ctrl + C in it).
Step 2: Double-click "Start-Borkeo-WiFi.bat". At the first start the new tables
        (ANC pregnancy record, ANC visit tasks) are added to the database. No
        patient data and no old ANC form is touched.
Step 3: In the browser press Ctrl + F5 once. Triage now offers ANC, PNC, family
        planning, cervical screening, vaccination and safe abortion.
Step 4: Log in as the midwife (or as the Superuser and switch to Midwife). The
        new menu item "បញ្ជីអ្នកជំងឺសេវាមាតា" appears. Open a patient and the
        official ANC record opens; the to-do side panel shows the tasks of the
        current visit.

For Antigravity (only when applying to another copy of v42.5.0):
  a) copy the three ANC catalogue files exactly from this folder (they are the
     official files, copied unchanged; do not retype):
       server/catalogues/anc-color-chart.json
         sha256 8cdf86f303bb09e3b2be423fe36023971aded4b162f97572f8129e54d31fa0fc
       server/catalogues/anc-visit-schedule.json
         sha256 81c505e83cbf11e4c49b26b6e9e1a44c675661a0342b9944ba3ef52642fd8bb2
       server/catalogues/anc-record-labels.json
         sha256 1f1f9c51ad2b271c431ff3c1f64deb826f0decbd61f01bc3b6683a0fc2f8f650
  b) apply the code change in section 5 exactly (these three data files are not
     repeated in it), then run "npm test" and "npm run check:khmer".

5. CODE CHANGE (unified diff against v42.5.0; the three ANC catalogue files of
   step 4 a are not repeated here)

+- Routing: ANC (anc), PNC (pnc), family planning (family-planning) and cervical screening (cervix-screening) go Registration -> Triage -> Cashier -> midwife; vaccination (vaccination) goes Triage -> midwife directly and is free (no Cashier); safe abortion (safe-abortion) goes to the Gynecology ward queue instead of the OPD doctor. The official boxes បៃតង / លឿង / ក្រហម show at the top of the record and as a badge in the patient banner on every page; every colour change is kept with its date, reasons and author.

 ## v42.5.0 (Medicine list in two steps, filtered for the patient; maximum dose check for adults and children)
 - Medicine list: server/catalogues/medicines-eml-2024.json is now the owner's new file (704 lines, 339 medicine names, with other names, route, adult / child / women-only / newborn-only marks, age limits and notes). Names no longer carry the other name in brackets ("Paracetamol", other name "acetaminophen").
 - Two-step search in every prescription form (OPD, IPD admission and protocol rows, daily note, discharge): step 1 shows each medicine name once (name, other names, CMS code; "para" shows only Paracetamol, "acetam" also finds it, "cotrim" finds Sulfamethoxazole + trimethoprim); step 2 shows its forms and strengths as "CMS code · Form · Strength" with any note as an orange line (⚠ កុមារ៖ ..., ⚠ មនុស្សពេញវ័យ៖ ...). A single allowed line is chosen automatically; a full CMS code (e.g. AA0410) goes straight to its line. Arrows and Enter in both steps, Backspace from step 2 back to the names, Esc closes. The chosen line fills CMS-Name-Form-Strength, clears and focuses the number of medicine and sets the unit; the route is never filled; free text stays allowed.

+# ANC Khmer to confirm (v42.6.0)
+
+The Khmer of the official ANC record (pages 2-5) was transcribed from the page
+images in `borkeo-update-files/anc/moh-2026-pages/` into
+`server/catalogues/anc-record-labels.json`. The colour chart and the visit
+schedule were copied unchanged from `anc-color-chart.json` and
+`anc-visit-schedule.json`. Please check the words below against the paper
+record; correct them in `server/catalogues/anc-record-labels.json` (the page
+reads them from there) and in `docs/approved-source-khmer.json`.
+
+## Label chosen without a source (owner instruction)
+
+| Where | Khmer used | Meaning |
+|---|---|---|
+| Triage service choice, vaccination page title | ចាក់វ៉ាក់សាំង | Vaccination (free, by the midwife) |
+
+## Words not read with certainty on the page images
+
+| Page | Label key | Khmer used | Doubt |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| 2 | previous.complicationList 2 | បេរីណេមានស្នាមថ្មេរ មានផ្លែ ដុះសាច់/សិរមាន់ | The image reads "បេរីណេ"; the colour chart writes "ប៉េរីណេ". |
+| 2 | previous.complicationList 9 | ធ្លាប់មានកូនកើតមកមានទំងន់ទាបធៀបនឹងអាយុគភ៌ | Small print: "ទំងន់" (the rest of the record writes "ទម្ងន់") and "នឹង" / "និង". |
+| 2 | allergy.title | ប្រវត្តិអាល្លែកហ្ស៊ីថ្នាំ៖ | Spelling "អាល្លែកហ្ស៊ី" as on the image (the EMR elsewhere writes "អាលែកហ្ស៊ី"). |
+| 2 | family.items.mental | ការយឺតយ៉ាវបញ្ញាស្មារតី | Small print. |
+| 2 | gyn.cycle / gyn.duration | វដ្តរៀងរាល់ … ថ្ងៃ ចំនួនថ្ងៃ …/ប្រចាំខែ | Dotted line between the words; exact order of the words. |
+| 2 | violence.title | កត្តាប្រឈម អំពើហិង្សាពាក់ព័ន្ធសុខភាពបន្តពូជ ផ្លូវភេទ និងយេនឌ័រ៖ | Bold small print. |
+| 2 | identity.bmiNormal | (ទម្ងន់ធម្មតា 18.6-24.9) | The record prints 18.6; the colour chart uses 18.5. The colour code uses the chart (18.5). |
+| 3 | labs.hct | - អេម៉ាតូគ្រីត HCT | Small print. |
+| 3 | ultrasound columns | ទម្រង់បង្ហាញ · ក្បាលកូន · ទីតាំងសុក · ទឹកភ្លោះ · ទម្ងន់កូន | Underlined column headers. |
+| 3-5 | visits column varices | ឡើងសរសៃវ៉ែន/ហើម | Three short lines in the header ("ឡើង / សរសៃ / វ៉ែន/ហើម"). |
+| 3-5 | visits column diagnosis | មូលវិចារ (រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យ និងអន្តរាគមន៍) | The words in brackets are very small. |
+| all | footer | ការិយាល័យគិលានុបដ្ឋាក និងឆ្មបនៃនាយកដ្ឋានសេវាសុខភាព ឆ្នាំ២០២៦ | Small print at the bottom of each page. |
+
+## Notes on the official files (copied unchanged)
+
+- `anc-color-chart.json`: twins are in both yellow and red; the EMR uses red
+  (owner default, `levelOverrides` in `server/clinical-options.json`).
+- The chart says "BMI ធម្មតា (18.5-24.9)" while the record page prints 18.6.
+
+## Other new Khmer
+
+Every other new Khmer text of this update (buttons, messages, the to-do panel,
+the vaccination page, the referral letter) is listed in
+`docs/translations-needed.csv` with "draft Khmer, owner to review" in the
+context column. Change the khmer column there and run `npm run translations:apply`.

diff --git a/public/anc-engine.mjs b/public/anc-engine.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..76b82cc
--- /dev/null
+++ b/public/anc-engine.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,294 @@
+// v42.6 - ANC engine (no page code; used by the ANC record page, the server
+// and the tests). Rules come from the official files copied into
+// server/catalogues (anc-color-chart.json, anc-visit-schedule.json) and the
+// owner defaults in server/clinical-options.json "maternal".
+// - Dates are 'YYYY-MM-DD' strings; GA is in weeks (decimal) from the EDD.
+// - EDD = LMP + 280 days; a dating ultrasound before 14 weeks whose EDD is more
+//   than 7 days different replaces it (the source is always returned).
+// - Colour: every chart item is checked (automatic items from the data, the
+//   others from tick boxes); the highest level wins: red > yellow > green.
+// - Schedule: green = 4 visits (page 7), yellow / red = 8 visits (page 8).
+
+export const DEFAULT_OPTIONS = Object.freeze({
+  datingUltrasoundMaxWeeks: 14,
+  datingUltrasoundMaxDifferenceDays: 7,
+  prePregnancyWeightFromTriageMaxWeeks: 12,
+  singleWeekVisitTolerance: 1,
+  levelOverrides: { twins_y: 'red' },
+  heightCheckBelowCm: 145,
+});
+
+const DAY = 86400000;
+const LEVELS = { green: 0, yellow: 1, red: 2 };
+
+export function parseDate(value) {
+  const match = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/.exec(String(value || ''));
+  if (!match) return null;
+  const time = Date.UTC(Number(match[1]), Number(match[2]) - 1, Number(match[3]));
+  return Number.isNaN(time) ? null : time;
+}
+
+export function formatDate(time) {
+  return new Date(time).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+}
+
+export function addDays(value, days) {
+  const time = parseDate(value);
+  return time === null ? '' : formatDate(time + Math.round(days) * DAY);
+}
+
+export function daysBetween(from, to) {
+  const a = parseDate(from), b = parseDate(to);
+  return a === null || b === null ? null : Math.round((b - a) / DAY);
+}
+
+export function eddFromLmp(lmp) {
+  return parseDate(lmp) === null ? '' : addDays(lmp, 280);
+}
+
+// The EDD used by the record and where it comes from ('lmp' | 'ultrasound' | '').
+export function datingEdd({ lmp = '', ultrasoundEdd = '', ultrasoundDate = '' } = {}, options = DEFAULT_OPTIONS) {
+  const fromLmp = eddFromLmp(lmp);
+  const usEdd = parseDate(ultrasoundEdd) === null ? '' : String(ultrasoundEdd).slice(0, 10);
+  let usEarly = false;
+  if (usEdd && parseDate(ultrasoundDate) !== null) {
+    const gaDaysAtScan = 280 - daysBetween(ultrasoundDate, usEdd);
+    usEarly = gaDaysAtScan >= 0 && gaDaysAtScan < (options.datingUltrasoundMaxWeeks ?? 14) * 7;
+  }
+  if (fromLmp && usEdd && usEarly && Math.abs(daysBetween(fromLmp, usEdd)) > (options.datingUltrasoundMaxDifferenceDays ?? 7)) {
+    return { edd: usEdd, source: 'ultrasound', lmpEdd: fromLmp };
+  }
+  if (fromLmp) return { edd: fromLmp, source: 'lmp', lmpEdd: fromLmp };
+  if (usEdd) return { edd: usEdd, source: 'ultrasound', lmpEdd: '' };
+  return { edd: '', source: '', lmpEdd: '' };
+}
+
+// GA on a date from the EDD: { weeks (decimal), completedWeeks, days, label '27+3' }.
+export function gestationalAge(edd, date) {
+  const left = daysBetween(date, edd);
+  if (left === null) return null;
+  const totalDays = 280 - left;
+  if (totalDays < 0) return null;
+  return { weeks: totalDays / 7, completedWeeks: Math.floor(totalDays / 7), days: totalDays % 7, label: `${Math.floor(totalDays / 7)}+${totalDays % 7}` };
+}
+
+// Date when the pregnancy reaches a GA (weeks).
+export function dateAtGa(edd, weeks) {
+  return parseDate(edd) === null ? '' : addDays(edd, weeks * 7 - 280);
+}
+
+export function bmi(weightKg, heightCm) {
+  const w = Number(weightKg), h = Number(heightCm) / 100;
+  if (!(w > 0) || !(h > 0)) return null;
+  return Math.round((w / (h * h)) * 10) / 10;
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- colour
+const num = (value) => {
+  if (value === null || value === undefined || String(value).trim() === '') return null;
+  const n = Number(String(value).replace(',', '.'));
+  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
+};
+
+export function parseBp(value) {
+  const match = /(\d{2,3})\s*\/\s*(\d{2,3})/.exec(String(value || ''));
+  return match ? { systolic: Number(match[1]), diastolic: Number(match[2]) } : null;
+}
+
+// 'positive' | 'negative' | '' from a typed or released result.
+export function resultSign(value) {
+  const text = String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
+  if (!text) return '';
+  if (/non[\s-]?reactive|negative|neg\b|\u17a2\u179c\u17b7\u1787\u17d2\u1787\u1798\u17b6\u1793|^-$|^\(-\)$/.test(text)) return 'negative';
+  if (/reactive|positive|pos\b|\u179c\u17b7\u1787\u17d2\u1787\u1798\u17b6\u1793|^\+$|^\(\+\)$/.test(text)) return 'positive';
+  return '';
+}
+
+// Facts the automatic chart items use. record = the ANC record; visits = saved
+// visits (oldest first); today = date of the check.
+export function ancFacts(record = {}, visits = [], today = '') {
+  const identity = record.identity || {};
+  const previous = Array.isArray(record.previous) ? record.previous.filter((row) => row && Object.values(row).some(Boolean)) : [];
+  const labs = Array.isArray(record.labs) ? record.labs : [];
+  const latest = (pick) => {
+    for (const source of [...visits].reverse()) { const value = pick(source); if (value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== '') return value; }
+    return null;
+  };
+  const latestLab = (key) => {
+    const fromTable = [...labs].filter((column) => column && String(column[key] ?? '').trim()).sort((a, b) => String(a.date || '').localeCompare(String(b.date || ''))).pop();
+    const fromVisit = latest((visit) => visit?.rapid?.[key]);
+    const tableDate = fromTable?.date || '';
+    const visitDate = latest((visit) => (visit?.rapid?.[key] ? visit.date : null)) || '';
+    if (fromTable && (!fromVisit || tableDate >= visitDate)) return fromTable[key];
+    return fromVisit;
+  };
+  const dating = datingEdd({ lmp: record.lmp, ultrasoundEdd: record.ultrasoundEdd, ultrasoundDate: record.ultrasoundDate });
+  const ga = today && dating.edd ? gestationalAge(dating.edd, today) : null;
+  const bp = parseBp(latest((visit) => visit?.bp));
+  const births = num(identity.births);
+  const miscarriages = num(identity.miscarriages);
+  const abortions = num(identity.abortions);
+  const caesareanRows = previous.filter((row) => /caesarean|c-section|\u179c\u17c7\u1780\u17b6\u178f\u17cb/i.test(String(row.deliveryType || ''))).length;
+  const lastBirth = previous.map((row) => row.date).filter((date) => parseDate(date) !== null).sort().pop();
+  const monthsSince = lastBirth && today ? Math.floor(daysBetween(lastBirth, today) / 30.4375) : null;
+  const group = String(latestLab('group') || '');
+  return {
+    age: num(identity.age),
+    heightCm: num(record.heightCm),
+    prePregnancyBmi: bmi(record.prePregnancyWeightKg, record.heightCm),
+    bp,
+    births,
+    miscarriages,
+    abortionsMiscarriages: births === null && miscarriages === null && abortions === null ? null : (miscarriages || 0) + (abortions || 0),
+    caesareans: Math.max(caesareanRows, num(identity.caesareans) || 0) || (previous.length ? 0 : null),
+    monthsSinceLastBirth: monthsSince,
+    gaWeeks: ga ? ga.weeks : null,
+    hb: num(latestLab('hb')),
+    hiv: resultSign(latestLab('hiv')),
+    syphilis: resultSign(latestLab('syphilis')),
+    hbsag: resultSign(latestLab('hbsag')),
+    rhesus: /neg|rh\s*-|[abo]+\s*-|\(-\)|\u17a2\u179c\u17b7\u1787\u17d2\u1787\u1798\u17b6\u1793/i.test(group) ? 'negative' : group ? 'positive' : '',
+  };
+}
+
+// Automatic items: true (applies), false (does not apply), null (unknown).
+const AUTO = {
+  age_normal: (f) => (f.age === null ? null : f.age >= 20 && f.age <= 34),
+  age_risk: (f) => (f.age === null ? null : f.age < 20 || f.age > 34),
+  height_normal: (f) => (f.heightCm === null ? null : f.heightCm > 150),
+  height_risk: (f) => (f.heightCm === null ? null : f.heightCm <= 150),
+  bmi_normal: (f) => (f.prePregnancyBmi === null ? null : f.prePregnancyBmi >= 18.5 && f.prePregnancyBmi < 25),
+  bmi_risk: (f) => (f.prePregnancyBmi === null ? null : f.prePregnancyBmi < 18.5 || (f.prePregnancyBmi >= 25 && f.prePregnancyBmi <= 30)),
+  bmi_high: (f) => (f.prePregnancyBmi === null ? null : f.prePregnancyBmi > 30),
+  bp_normal: (f) => (f.bp ? f.bp.systolic < 140 && f.bp.diastolic < 90 : null),
+  bp_risk: (f) => (f.bp ? (f.bp.systolic >= 140 || f.bp.diastolic >= 90) && f.bp.systolic < 160 && f.bp.diastolic < 110 : null),
+  bp_severe: (f) => (f.bp ? f.bp.systolic >= 160 || f.bp.diastolic >= 110 : null),
+  anaemia_severe: (f) => (f.hb === null ? null : f.hb < 7),
+  hb_moderate: (f) => (f.hb === null ? null : f.hb >= 7 && f.hb < 10),
+  hb_normal: (f) => (f.hb === null ? null : f.hb >= 10),
+  births_normal: (f) => (f.births === null ? null : f.births <= 3),
+  many_children: (f) => (f.births === null ? null : f.births === 4),
+  grand_multipara: (f) => (f.births === null ? null : f.births >= 5),
+  no_abortion: (f) => (f.abortionsMiscarriages === null ? null : f.abortionsMiscarriages === 0),
+  abortion_1: (f) => (f.abortionsMiscarriages === null ? null : f.abortionsMiscarriages >= 1),
+  miscarriage_3: (f) => (f.miscarriages === null ? null : f.miscarriages >= 3),
+  interval_ok: (f) => (f.monthsSinceLastBirth === null ? null : f.monthsSinceLastBirth > 24),
+  interval_short: (f) => (f.monthsSinceLastBirth === null ? null : f.monthsSinceLastBirth <= 24),
+  csection_1: (f) => (f.caesareans === null ? null : f.caesareans === 1),
+  csection_2: (f) => (f.caesareans === null ? null : f.caesareans >= 2),
+  post_term: (f) => (f.gaWeeks === null ? null : f.gaWeeks >= 41),
+  infections_negative: (f) => ([f.hiv, f.syphilis, f.hbsag].every((v) => v === 'negative')),
+  not_tested: (f) => ([f.hiv, f.syphilis, f.hbsag].some((v) => !v)),
+  infection_positive: (f) => ([f.hiv, f.syphilis, f.hbsag].some((v) => v === 'positive')),
+  rh_negative: (f) => (f.rhesus ? f.rhesus === 'negative' : null),
+};
+
+// { colour, reasons: [{ key, level, kh, auto }], flags: [] }. ticks: { itemKey: true }.
+export function evaluateColour(chart, facts, ticks = {}, options = DEFAULT_OPTIONS) {
+  const reasons = [];
+  const flags = [];
+  for (const section of chart?.sections || []) {
+    for (const item of section.items || []) {
+      const level = options.levelOverrides?.[item.key] || item.level;
+      const rule = AUTO[item.key];
+      const automatic = rule && item.auto ? rule(facts) : null;
+      const applies = automatic === true || Boolean(ticks[item.key]);
+      if (applies && level !== 'green') reasons.push({ key: item.key, level, kh: item.kh, section: section.key, auto: automatic === true });
+    }
+  }
+  if (facts.heightCm !== null && facts.heightCm < (options.heightCheckBelowCm ?? 145)) flags.push('height-check');
+  const colour = reasons.reduce((best, reason) => (LEVELS[reason.level] > LEVELS[best] ? reason.level : best), 'green');
+  return { colour, reasons, flags };
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- schedule
+export function planFor(colour, schedule) {
+  return colour === 'green' ? schedule.green : schedule.yellowRed;
+}
+
+const CATEGORIES = ['records', 'lab', 'exam', 'ultrasound', 'care'];
+
+// What a task does: own result field, a Laboratory / Imaging order, the iron
+// prescription, the tetanus vaccine, or a simple check.
+export function taskAction(category, text) {
+  const value = String(text || '');
+  if (category === 'ultrasound') return /^\*/.test(value) ? 'check' : 'order:ULTRASOUND';
+  if (category === 'lab') {
+    if (/Hepatitis B/i.test(value)) return 'order:HBSAG';
+    if (/OGTT/i.test(value)) return 'order:OGTT';
+    if (/CBC/i.test(value)) return 'own:hb';
+    if (/^HIV$/i.test(value.trim())) return 'own:hiv';
+    if (/Syphilis/i.test(value)) return 'own:syphilis';
+    if (/\u1780\u17d2\u179a\u17bb\u1798\u1788\u17b6\u1798/.test(value)) return 'order:BLOOD_GROUP';
+    if (/\u1791\u17b9\u1780\u1793\u17c4\u1798/.test(value)) return 'own:urine';
+    return 'check';
+  }
+  if (category === 'care') {
+    if (/\u1787\u17b6\u178f\u17b7\u178a\u17c2\u1780/.test(value)) return 'rx:iron';
+    if (/\u178f\u17c1\u178f\u17b6\u178e\u17bc\u179f/.test(value)) return 'vaccine:tt';
+    return 'check';
+  }
+  return 'check';
+}
+
+export function tasksFor(visit) {
+  if (!visit) return [];
+  return CATEGORIES.flatMap((category) => (visit[category] || []).map((text, index) => ({
+    key: `${visit.visit}:${category}:${index}`, visit: visit.visit, category, text, action: taskAction(category, text),
+  })));
+}
+
+// Automatically done from the data (results typed or released).
+export function taskDoneByData(task, facts, visit) {
+  if (task.action === 'own:hiv') return Boolean(facts.hiv);
+  if (task.action === 'own:syphilis') return Boolean(facts.syphilis);
+  if (task.action === 'order:HBSAG') return Boolean(facts.hbsag);
+  if (task.action === 'own:hb') return facts.hb !== null;
+  if (task.action === 'order:BLOOD_GROUP') return Boolean(facts.rhesus);
+  if (task.action === 'own:urine') return Boolean(visit && (visit.proteinuria || visit.glucosuria));
+  return false;
+}
+
+// The visit for a GA: { visit, next, scheduled }. doneVisits: visit numbers already seen.
+export function visitForGa(plan, gaWeeks, doneVisits = []) {
+  if (!plan || gaWeeks === null || gaWeeks === undefined) return { visit: null, next: plan?.visits?.[0] || null, scheduled: false };
+  const week = Math.floor(gaWeeks);
+  const matches = plan.visits.filter((visit) => week >= visit.gaFrom && week <= visit.gaTo);
+  const visit = matches.find((item) => !doneVisits.includes(item.visit)) || matches[matches.length - 1] || null;
+  const next = plan.visits.find((item) => item.gaFrom > week && (!visit || item.visit > visit.visit)) || null;
+  return { visit, next, scheduled: Boolean(visit) };
+}
+
+// Tasks of earlier visits that are not done and not closed with a reason.
+export function overdueTasks(plan, gaWeeks, taskState = {}, facts = {}) {
+  if (!plan || gaWeeks === null || gaWeeks === undefined) return [];
+  const week = Math.floor(gaWeeks);
+  return plan.visits.filter((visit) => visit.gaTo < week).flatMap(tasksFor)
+    .filter((task) => !taskState[task.key] && !taskDoneByData(task, facts, null) && task.category !== 'records');
+}
+
+// Suggested date of the next visit (first day of its weeks); '' when none.
+export function suggestedAppointment(edd, nextVisit) {
+  return nextVisit ? dateAtGa(edd, nextVisit.gaFrom) : '';
+}
+
+// One summary for the page and the server.
+export function ancStatus({ chart, schedule, record = {}, visits = [], today, options = DEFAULT_OPTIONS }) {
+  const facts = ancFacts(record, visits, today);
+  const colour = evaluateColour(chart, facts, record.ticks || {}, options);
+  const dating = datingEdd({ lmp: record.lmp, ultrasoundEdd: record.ultrasoundEdd, ultrasoundDate: record.ultrasoundDate }, options);
+  const ga = dating.edd ? gestationalAge(dating.edd, today) : null;
+  const plan = planFor(colour.colour, schedule);
+  const doneVisits = visits.map((visit) => visit.scheduleVisit).filter(Boolean);
+  // GA not known yet (no LMP): the tasks of the first visit.
+  const placed = ga ? visitForGa(plan, ga.weeks, doneVisits)
+    : { visit: plan?.visits?.[0] || null, next: plan?.visits?.[1] || null, scheduled: Boolean(plan?.visits?.length) };
+  const tasks = tasksFor(placed.visit).map((task) => ({ ...task, state: record.tasks?.[task.key] || null, autoDone: taskDoneByData(task, facts, visits[visits.length - 1]) }));
+  return {
+    facts, colour: colour.colour, reasons: colour.reasons, flags: colour.flags, dating, ga,
+    plan: colour.colour === 'green' ? 'green' : 'yellowRed', visitsPlanned: plan?.visits?.length || 0,
+    visit: placed.visit, next: placed.next, scheduled: placed.scheduled, tasks,
+    overdue: overdueTasks(plan, ga ? ga.weeks : null, record.tasks || {}, facts),
+    appointment: suggestedAppointment(dating.edd, placed.next),
+  };
+}

+// v42.6 - The official ANC record page (/anc-record?patient=...).
+// One record per pregnancy (linked to the Health ID), reused at every visit
+// until delivery, referral to CPA3 or closing. Pages 2-5 of the Ministry of
+// Health record, today's visit (Triage values imported, editable), the colour
+// code with its history, the visit schedule and the "to do at this visit"
+// panel, the referral to CPA3 and printing (A4 landscape).
+import * as anc from '/public/anc-engine.mjs';
+import { referralLetterHtml } from '/public/anc-referral-letter.mjs';
+
+const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
+const esc = (value) => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
+const english = () => window.getBorkeoLanguage?.() === 'en';
+// New UI text: approved Khmer (public/new-text-data.js) or the English placeholder.
+const t = (key, en) => (english() ? en : (window.newText ? window.newText(key, en) : en));
+const patientKey = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('patient') || '';
+let cfg = null;          // GET /api/maternal/config
+let data = null;         // GET /api/anc/record
+let record = null;       // working copy of the record (pages 2-5)
+let draft = {};          // today's visit
+let prescription = [];   // today's prescription lines
+let tasksShown = false;
+let csrf = '';
+
+async function api(path, body) {
+  if (body && !csrf) csrf = (await fetch('/api/auth/me', { credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'no-store' }).then((r) => r.json())).csrfToken;
+  const response = await fetch(path, body ? { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': csrf }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }
+    : { credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'no-store' });
+  const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
+  if (!response.ok) throw Object.assign(new Error(result.error || String(response.status)), { body: result, status: response.status });
+  return result;
+}
+
+const L = () => cfg.labels;
+const setStatus = (text, error = false) => { const node = $('anc-status'); node.textContent = text || ''; node.dataset.state = error ? 'error' : 'ok'; };
+const canEdit = () => Boolean(data?.canEdit && data?.pregnancy?.status === 'open');
+const colourWord = (colour) => L().colours[colour] || colour;
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- paths
+function getPath(object, path) {
+  return path.split('.').reduce((value, key) => (value == null ? undefined : value[key]), object);
+}
+function setPath(object, path, value) {
+  const keys = path.split('.');
+  let node = object;
+  keys.slice(0, -1).forEach((key, index) => {
+    if (node[key] == null || typeof node[key] !== 'object') node[key] = /^\d+$/.test(keys[index + 1]) ? [] : {};
+    node = node[key];
+  });
+  node[keys[keys.length - 1]] = value;
+}
+const field = (path, { type = 'text', size = '', placeholder = '' } = {}) => {
+  const value = getPath(record, path);
+  if (type === 'checkbox') return `<input type="checkbox" data-path="${esc(path)}"${value ? ' checked' : ''}${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>`;
+  return `<input type="${type}" data-path="${esc(path)}" value="${esc(value ?? '')}" class="anc-field ${size}" placeholder="${esc(placeholder)}"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>`;
+};
+const choice = (path, value, label) => `<label class="anc-choice"><input type="radio" name="${esc(path)}" data-path="${esc(path)}" value="${esc(value)}"${getPath(record, path) === value ? ' checked' : ''}${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}> ${esc(label)}</label>`;
+const vfield = (key, { type = 'text', size = '' } = {}) => `<input type="${type}" data-visit="${esc(key)}" value="${esc(getPath(draft, key) ?? '')}" class="anc-field ${size}"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>`;
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- status
+function visitsWithDraft() {
+  const saved = (data?.visits || []).filter((visit) => !(visit.date === data.today && visit.patientKey === patientKey));
+  return [...saved, { ...draft, date: data.today, patientKey }].sort((a, b) => String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));
+}
+function currentStatus() {
+  const labs = [...(data.released?.labs || []), ...(record.labs || [])];
+  return anc.ancStatus({ chart: cfg.chart, schedule: cfg.schedule, record: { ...record, labs }, visits: visitsWithDraft(), today: data.today, options: cfg.options });
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- top and summary
+function renderTop(status) {
+  $('anc-title').textContent = L().title;
+  document.title = L().title;
+  const colour = status?.colour || '';
+  $('anc-colour-boxes').innerHTML = ['green', 'yellow', 'red'].map((key) => `<span class="anc-colour-box anc-box-${key}${colour === key ? ' is-ticked' : ''}" data-colour="${key}"><span class="anc-box" aria-hidden="true">${colour === key ? '✓' : ''}</span>${esc(colourWord(key))}</span>`).join('');
+}
+
+function renderSummary(status) {
+  const host = $('anc-summary');
+  if (!data.pregnancy) { host.innerHTML = ''; return; }
+  const ga = status.ga ? `${status.ga.label} ${t('anc.weeks', 'weeks')}` : '—';
+  const source = status.dating.source === 'ultrasound' ? t('anc.sourceUltrasound', 'from the dating ultrasound') : status.dating.source === 'lmp' ? t('anc.sourceLmp', 'from the last menstrual period') : '';
+  const colourNote = status.colour === 'yellow' ? t('anc.yellowNote', 'ANC continues at Borkeo Referral Hospital (CPA2) - 8 visits')
+    : status.colour === 'red' ? t('anc.redNote', 'Refer to the provincial hospital (CPA3) with a referral form signed by a doctor') : t('anc.greenNote', 'ANC with the midwife - 4 visits');
+  const pendingReferral = (data.visits || []).find((visit) => visit.referral?.requestedAt && !visit.referral?.signed);
+  const signedReferral = [...(data.visits || [])].reverse().find((visit) => visit.referral?.signed);
+  const closed = data.pregnancy.status !== 'open' ? `<span class="anc-closed">${esc(t(`anc.status.${data.pregnancy.status}`, data.pregnancy.status))}</span>` : '';
+  host.innerHTML = `
+    <div class="anc-summary-line"><strong>${esc(data.patient.fullName)}</strong> · ${esc(data.patient.healthId || data.patient.caseNumber || '')} ${closed}</div>
+    <div class="anc-summary-line">${esc(L().identity.edd)} <strong>${esc(status.dating.edd || '—')}</strong> <small>${esc(source)}</small> · GA <strong>${esc(ga)}</strong></div>
+    <div class="anc-colour-note anc-note-${esc(status.colour)}">${esc(colourNote)}${status.flags.includes('height-check') ? ` · ${esc(t('anc.heightCheck', 'Height under 145 cm: check'))}` : ''}</div>
+    ${status.reasons.length ? `<ul class="anc-reasons">${status.reasons.map((reason) => `<li class="anc-reason-${esc(reason.level)}">${esc(reason.kh)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>` : ''}
+    <div class="anc-actions no-print">
+      ${canEdit() ? `<button type="button" data-action="save-record">${esc(t('anc.saveRecord', 'Save the record'))}</button>` : ''}
+      <button type="button" class="secondary" data-action="print">${esc(t('form.print', 'Print'))}</button>
+      ${canEdit() && status.colour === 'red' && !pendingReferral ? `<button type="button" class="danger" data-action="referral">${esc(t('anc.requestReferral', 'Refer to CPA3 (doctor signs)'))}</button>` : ''}
+      ${pendingReferral && data.canSignReferral ? `<button type="button" class="danger" data-action="sign">${esc(t('anc.signReferral', 'Sign the referral'))}</button>` : ''}
+      ${pendingReferral && !data.canSignReferral ? `<span class="anc-pending">${esc(t('anc.referralPending', 'Referral waiting for a doctor signature'))}</span>` : ''}
+      ${signedReferral ? `<button type="button" class="secondary" data-action="print-referral">${esc(t('anc.printReferral', 'Print the referral letter'))}</button>` : ''}
+      ${canEdit() ? `<button type="button" class="secondary" data-action="close">${esc(t('anc.closeRecord', 'Close the record'))}</button>` : ''}
+    </div>`;
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- page 2
+function page2() {
+  const l = L();
+  const id = l.identity;
+  const yesNo = (path) => `${choice(path, 'no', l.gyn.none)} ${choice(path, 'yes', l.gyn.yes)} ${field(`${path}Text`, { size: 'wide' })}`;
+  const bmiValue = anc.bmi(record.prePregnancyWeightKg, record.heightCm);
+  return `<section class="anc-page" data-page="2">
+    <h2>${esc(l.title)}</h2>
+    <div class="anc-box-panel anc-identity">
+      <label>${esc(id.name)} ${field('identity.name', { size: 'wide' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.dateOfBirth)} ${field('identity.dateOfBirth', { type: 'date' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.age)} ${field('identity.age', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.gravida)} ${field('identity.gravida', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.births)} ${field('identity.births', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.miscarriages)} ${field('identity.miscarriages', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.abortions)} ${field('identity.abortions', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.lmp)} ${field('lmp', { type: 'date' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.edd)} <output>${esc(anc.eddFromLmp(record.lmp) || '—')}</output></label>
+      <label>${esc(id.eddUltrasound)} ${field('ultrasoundEdd', { type: 'date' })} <small>${esc(t('anc.scanDate', 'scan date'))}</small> ${field('ultrasoundDate', { type: 'date' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.height)} ${field('heightCm', { size: 'tiny' })} ${esc(id.heightUnit)}</label>
+      <label>${esc(id.weightBefore)} ${field('prePregnancyWeightKg', { size: 'tiny' })} kg <small>${esc(record.prePregnancyWeightSource === 'triage' ? t('anc.fromTriage', 'from Triage') : '')}</small></label>
+      <label>${esc(id.bmiBefore)} <output>${esc(bmiValue ?? '—')}</output> ${esc(id.bmiNormal)}</label>
+    </div>
+    <div class="anc-two">
+      <div class="anc-box-panel">
+        <p><strong>• ${esc(l.general.title)}</strong> ${Object.entries(l.general.items).map(([key, label]) => `<label class="anc-choice">${field(`history.general.${key}`, { type: 'checkbox' })} ${esc(label)}</label>`).join(' ')} ${field('history.general.other', { size: 'wide' })}</p>
+        <p><strong>• ${esc(l.surgery.title)}</strong></p>
+        ${l.surgery.numbers.map((number, index) => `<p class="anc-indent">${esc(number)} ${esc(l.surgery.diagnosis)} ${field(`history.surgery.${index}.diagnosis`, { size: 'wide' })} ${esc(l.surgery.year)} ${field(`history.surgery.${index}.year`, { size: 'tiny' })} ${esc(l.surgery.place)} ${field(`history.surgery.${index}.place`)}</p>`).join('')}
+        <p><strong>• ${esc(l.allergy.title)}</strong></p>
+        ${l.allergy.numbers.map((number, index) => `<p class="anc-indent">${esc(number)} ${esc(l.allergy.drug)} ${field(`history.allergy.${index}.drug`)} ${esc(l.allergy.symptom)} ${field(`history.allergy.${index}.symptom`, { size: 'wide' })}</p>`).join('')}
+        <p class="anc-hint">${esc(t('anc.allergyLink', 'Drug allergies are also kept in the structured allergy record (patient banner).'))} ${(data.allergies || []).map((a) => esc(a)).join(', ')}</p>
+        <p><strong>• ${esc(l.family.title)}</strong> ${Object.entries(l.family.items).map(([key, label]) => `<label class="anc-choice">${field(`history.family.${key}`, { type: 'checkbox' })} ${esc(label)}</label>`).join(' ')} ${field('history.family.otherText', { size: 'wide' })}</p>
+        <p><strong>• ${esc(l.gyn.title)}</strong> ${esc(l.gyn.menses)} ${choice('history.gyn.menses', 'regular', l.gyn.regular)} ${choice('history.gyn.menses', 'irregular', l.gyn.irregular)}
+          ${esc(l.gyn.cycle)} ${field('history.gyn.cycleDays', { size: 'tiny' })} ${esc(l.gyn.day)} ${esc(l.gyn.duration)} ${field('history.gyn.durationDays', { size: 'tiny' })}${esc(l.gyn.perMonth)}</p>
+        <p class="anc-indent">${esc(l.gyn.contraception)} ${choice('history.gyn.contraception', 'no', l.gyn.none)} (${esc(l.gyn.reason)} ${field('history.gyn.contraceptionReason')}) ${choice('history.gyn.contraception', 'yes', l.gyn.yes)} ${field('history.gyn.contraceptionText')}</p>
+        <p class="anc-indent">${esc(l.gyn.art)} ${choice('history.gyn.art', 'no', l.gyn.none)} ${choice('history.gyn.art', 'yes', l.gyn.yes)} ${esc(l.gyn.otherGyn)} ${field('history.gyn.otherGyn', { size: 'wide' })}</p>
+      </div>
+      <div>
+        <div class="anc-box-panel">
+          <p><strong>• ${esc(l.social.title)}</strong> ${['smoking', 'alcohol', 'drugs'].map((key) => `<label class="anc-choice">${field(`history.social.${key}`, { type: 'checkbox' })} ${esc(l.social[key])}</label>`).join(' ')} ${field('history.social.other')}</p>
+          <p><strong>• ${esc(l.violence.title)}</strong> ${yesNo('history.violence')}</p>
+        </div>
+        <div class="anc-box-panel">
+          <p><strong>• ${esc(l.mouth.title)}</strong></p>
+          ${['stomatitis', 'caries', 'breast'].map((key) => `<p class="anc-indent">${esc(l.mouth[key])} ${yesNo(`history.mouth.${key}`)}</p>`).join('')}
+          <p><strong>• ${esc(l.anaemia.title)}</strong></p>
+          ${['eyelid', 'nails', 'palms'].map((key) => `<p class="anc-indent">${esc(l.anaemia[key])} ${yesNo(`history.anaemia.${key}`)}</p>`).join('')}
+        </div>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+    ${previousTable()}
+  </section>`;
+}

+function previousTable() {
+  const p = L().previous;
+  const rows = Array.from({ length: Math.max(5, (record.previous || []).length + 1) }, (_, index) => index);
+  return `<div class="anc-box-panel"><h3>${esc(p.title)}</h3><div class="anc-table-wrap"><table class="anc-table">
+    <thead><tr><th rowspan="2">${esc(p.number)}</th><th rowspan="2">${esc(p.date)}</th><th colspan="2">${esc(p.abortionGroup)}</th><th colspan="5">${esc(p.motherGroup)}</th><th colspan="4">${esc(p.babyGroup)}</th></tr>
+    <tr><th>${esc(p.under12)}</th><th>${esc(p.over12)}</th><th>${esc(p.term)}</th><th>${esc(p.deliveryType)}</th><th>${esc(p.place)}</th><th>${esc(p.complications)}</th><th>${esc(p.healthProblems)}</th>
+    <th>${esc(p.babyOutcome)}</th><th>${esc(p.sex)}</th><th>${esc(p.weight)}</th><th>${esc(p.babyProblems)}</th></tr></thead>
+    <tbody>${rows.map((index) => `<tr><td>${index + 1}</td><td>${field(`previous.${index}.date`, { type: 'date' })}</td>
+      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.under12`, { type: 'checkbox' })}</td><td>${field(`previous.${index}.over12`, { type: 'checkbox' })}</td>
+      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.term`, { size: 'small' })}</td>
+      <td><select data-path="previous.${index}.deliveryType"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>${['', 'normal', 'caesarean', 'vacuum'].map((value) => `<option value="${value}"${getPath(record, `previous.${index}.deliveryType`) === value ? ' selected' : ''}>${esc(value ? t(`anc.delivery.${value}`, value) : '')}</option>`).join('')}</select></td>
+      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.place`, { size: 'small' })}</td>
+      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.complications`, { size: 'small', placeholder: '1-10' })}</td>
+      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.healthProblems`, { size: 'small' })}</td>
+      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.babyOutcome`, { size: 'small' })}</td><td>${field(`previous.${index}.sex`, { size: 'tiny' })}</td>
+      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.weight`, { size: 'tiny' })}</td><td>${field(`previous.${index}.babyProblems`, { size: 'small' })}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>
+    <p class="anc-footnote">*${p.complicationList.map((item, index) => `${index + 1}.${esc(item)}`).join(' ')}</p></div>`;
+}

+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- page 3
+function labTable() {
+  const l = L().labs;
+  const released = data.released?.labs || [];
+  const rapid = (data.visits || []).filter((visit) => visit.rapid && Object.values(visit.rapid).some(Boolean)).map((visit) => ({ date: visit.date, source: 'rapid', ...visit.rapid }));
+  const typed = Array.from({ length: Math.max(4, (record.labs || []).length) }, (_, index) => index);
+  const rows = [['hiv', l.hiv], ['syphilis', l.syphilis], ['hbsag', l.hbsag], ['hcv', l.hcv], ['hb', `${l.redCells} ${l.hb}`], ['hct', l.hct], ['plt', l.plt], ['group', l.group]];
+  const freeLabels = record.labFreeLabels || [];
+  const readCell = (column, key) => `<td class="anc-readonly anc-src-${esc(column.source)}">${esc(column[key] || '')}</td>`;
+  return `<div class="anc-box-panel"><h3>${esc(l.title)}</h3><div class="anc-table-wrap"><table class="anc-table anc-lab-table">
+    <tbody>
+      <tr><th>${esc(l.date)}</th>${released.map((c) => readCell(c, 'date')).join('')}${rapid.map((c) => readCell(c, 'date')).join('')}${typed.map((i) => `<td>${field(`labs.${i}.date`, { type: 'date' })}</td>`).join('')}</tr>
+      ${rows.map(([key, label]) => `<tr><th>${esc(label)}</th>${released.map((c) => readCell(c, key)).join('')}${rapid.map((c) => readCell(c, key)).join('')}${typed.map((i) => `<td>${field(`labs.${i}.${key}`, { size: 'small' })}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
+      ${[0, 1, 2].map((n) => `<tr><th>${field(`labFreeLabels.${n}`, { placeholder: '…' })}</th>${released.map(() => '<td></td>').join('')}${rapid.map(() => '<td></td>').join('')}${typed.map((i) => `<td>${field(`labs.${i}.free${n}`, { size: 'small' })}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
+    </tbody></table></div>
+    ${freeLabels.length ? '' : ''}<p class="anc-hint">${esc(t('anc.labsHint', 'Released Laboratory results and the midwife\'s rapid tests are filled in automatically.'))}</p></div>`;
+}
+
+function ultrasoundTable() {
+  const u = L().ultrasound;
+  const released = data.released?.ultrasounds || [];
+  const typed = Array.from({ length: Math.max(3, (record.ultrasounds || []).length + 1) }, (_, index) => index);
+  return `<div class="anc-box-panel"><h3>${esc(u.title)}</h3><div class="anc-table-wrap"><table class="anc-table">
+    <thead><tr>${u.columns.map((c) => `<th>${esc(c.kh)}<br><small>${esc(c.en)}</small></th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
+    <tbody>${released.map((row) => `<tr class="anc-readonly">${u.columns.map((c) => `<td>${esc(row[c.key] || '')}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
+    ${typed.map((i) => `<tr>${u.columns.map((c) => `<td>${field(`ultrasounds.${i}.${c.key}`, { type: ['date', 'edd'].includes(c.key) ? 'date' : 'text', size: 'small' })}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
+}
+
+function visitValue(visit, key, status) {
+  if (key === 'date') return visit.date;
+  if (key === 'ga') return visit.gaWeeks != null ? `${Math.floor(visit.gaWeeks)}+${Math.round((visit.gaWeeks % 1) * 7)}` : '';
+  if (key === 'bp') return [visit.bp, visit.pulse].filter(Boolean).join(' - ');
+  if (key === 'name') return visit.staffName || visit.createdBy || '';
+  if (key === 'vaccination') return (data.vaccinations || []).filter((v) => v.givenDate === visit.date).map((v) => `TT${v.doseNumber}`).join(', ');
+  if (key === 'wp') return visit.wp || weightGain(visit.weight);
+  return visit[key] ?? '';
+}
+function weightGain(weight) {
+  const before = Number(record.prePregnancyWeightKg), now = Number(weight);
+  return before > 0 && now > 0 ? String(Math.round((now - before) * 10) / 10) : '';
+}
+
+function visitTable() {
+  const columns = L().visits.columns;
+  return `<div class="anc-box-panel"><div class="anc-table-wrap"><table class="anc-table anc-visit-table">
+    <thead><tr>${columns.map((c) => `<th>${esc(c.kh)}<br><small>${esc(c.en)}</small></th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
+    <tbody>${(data.visits || []).map((visit) => `<tr class="${visit.referred ? 'anc-referred' : ''}">${columns.map((c) => `<td>${esc(visitValue(visit, c.key))}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')
+      || `<tr><td colspan="${columns.length}">—</td></tr>`}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- today's visit
+function visitForm(status) {
+  const cols = Object.fromEntries(L().visits.columns.map((c) => [c.key, c]));
+  const label = (key) => `${esc(cols[key].kh)} <small>${esc(cols[key].en)}</small>`;
+  const imported = data.imported || {};
+  const visitName = status.scheduled ? `${t('anc.visitOf', 'Visit')} ${status.visit.visit}/${status.visitsPlanned} · ${status.visit.label}` : t('anc.notScheduled', 'Not a scheduled visit');
+  const ironOptions = (cfg.midwifeMedicines || []).map((item) => `<option value="${esc(item.id)}">${esc(item.label)}</option>`).join('');
+  return `<section class="anc-page anc-visit" id="anc-visit-form">
+    <h2>${esc(t('anc.todayVisit', "Today's visit"))} · ${esc(data.today)} · <span class="anc-visit-name">${esc(visitName)}</span></h2>
+    <p class="anc-hint">${esc(t('anc.importedHint', 'Values from Registration and from Triage of this visit; the midwife can correct them (the Triage value stays saved).'))}
+      ${esc([imported.weightKg && `${imported.weightKg} kg`, imported.bp && `${imported.bp} mmHg`, imported.pulse && `${imported.pulse}/min`, imported.temperature && `${imported.temperature} °C`, imported.spo2 && `SpO2 ${imported.spo2}%`].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</p>
+    <div class="anc-visit-grid">
+      <label>${label('weight')} ${vfield('weight', { size: 'small' })} kg</label>
+      <label>${label('wp')} <output>${esc(weightGain(draft.weight) || '—')}</output></label>
+      <label>${label('bp')} ${vfield('bp', { size: 'small' })} - ${vfield('pulse', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${esc(t('anc.temperature', 'Temperature'))} ${vfield('temperature', { size: 'tiny' })} °C</label>
+      <label>SpO2 ${vfield('spo2', { size: 'tiny' })} %</label>
+      <label>${label('uh')} ${vfield('uh', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${label('fp')} ${vfield('fp', { size: 'small' })}</label>
+      <label>${label('fhr')} ${vfield('fhr', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${label('proteinuria')} ${vfield('proteinuria', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${label('glucosuria')} ${vfield('glucosuria', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+      <label>${label('varices')} ${vfield('varices', { size: 'small' })}</label>
+      <label>${label('fer')} ${vfield('fer', { size: 'tiny' })}</label>
+    </div>
+    <fieldset class="anc-rapid"><legend>${esc(t('anc.rapidTests', 'Rapid tests done by the midwife'))}</legend>
+      <label>HIV ${rapidSelect('hiv')}</label><label>Syphilis ${rapidSelect('syphilis')}</label><label>Hb ${vfield('rapid.hb', { size: 'tiny' })} g/dL</label>
+    </fieldset>
+    <div class="anc-visit-grid wide">
+      <label>${label('complaint')} ${vfield('complaint', { size: 'wide' })}</label>
+      <label>${label('diagnosis')} ${vfield('diagnosis', { size: 'wide' })}</label>
+      <label>${label('appointment')} ${vfield('appointment', { type: 'date' })} <small>${esc(t('anc.suggested', 'suggested'))}: ${esc(status.appointment || '—')}</small></label>
+      <label>${label('name')} <output>${esc(window.emrSession?.user?.displayName || '')}</output></label>
+    </div>
+    <fieldset class="anc-rx"><legend>${esc(t('anc.prescription', 'Prescription (maternal services medicines)'))}</legend>
+      <table class="anc-table"><tbody>${prescription.map((line, index) => `<tr><td>${esc(line.name)}</td><td><input class="anc-field tiny" data-rx="${index}" data-rx-key="doseAmount" value="${esc(line.doseAmount)}"> ${esc(line.unit)}</td>
+        <td><select data-rx="${index}" data-rx-key="scheduleType">${['qd_m', 'bid', 'tid'].map((value) => `<option value="${value}"${line.scheduleType === value ? ' selected' : ''}>${value.toUpperCase().replace('_M', '')}</option>`).join('')}</select></td>
+        <td><input class="anc-field tiny" data-rx="${index}" data-rx-key="d" value="${esc(line.d)}"> ${esc(t('anc.days', 'days'))}</td><td>${esc(rxTotal(line))}</td>
+        <td><button type="button" class="link" data-rx-remove="${index}">✕</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>
+      ${canEdit() ? `<select id="anc-rx-pick"><option value=""></option>${ironOptions}</select> <button type="button" class="secondary" data-action="rx-add">+</button>
+        <button type="button" data-action="rx-send"${prescription.length ? '' : ' disabled'}>${esc(t('anc.sendPharmacy', 'Send to Pharmacy'))}</button>` : ''}
+    </fieldset>
+    ${canEdit() ? `<div class="anc-actions"><button type="button" data-action="save-visit">${esc(t('anc.saveVisit', 'Save the visit'))}</button>
+      <a class="secondary-link" href="/vaccination?patient=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}">${esc(t('anc.giveVaccine', 'Open the vaccination'))}</a></div>` : ''}
+  </section>`;
+}
+function rapidSelect(key) {
+  const value = getPath(draft, `rapid.${key}`) || '';
+  return `<select data-visit="rapid.${key}"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>${[['', ''], ['Negative', t('anc.negative', 'Negative')], ['Positive', t('anc.positive', 'Positive')]].map(([v, text]) => `<option value="${v}"${value === v ? ' selected' : ''}>${esc(text)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
+}
+const PER_DAY = { qd_m: 1, bid: 2, tid: 3 };
+const rxTotal = (line) => { const total = Number(line.doseAmount) * (PER_DAY[line.scheduleType] || 1) * Number(line.d); return total > 0 ? String(total) : ''; };
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- colour chart and history
+function chartSection(status) {
+  const applied = new Set(status.reasons.map((reason) => reason.key));
+  return `<section class="anc-page anc-chart"><h2>${esc(cfg.chart.title)}</h2>
+    <div class="anc-chart-grid">${cfg.chart.sections.map((section) => `<div class="anc-box-panel"><h3>${esc(section.kh)}</h3>
+      ${['green', 'yellow', 'red'].map((level) => `<div class="anc-chart-level anc-level-${level}"><strong>${esc(cfg.chart.levels[level])}</strong>
+        ${section.items.filter((item) => item.level === level).map((item) => `<label class="anc-choice${applied.has(item.key) ? ' is-applied' : ''}">
+          <input type="checkbox" data-path="ticks.${esc(item.key)}"${record.ticks?.[item.key] ? ' checked' : ''}${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}> ${esc(item.kh)}${item.auto ? ` <small class="anc-auto">${esc(t('anc.auto', 'automatic'))}</small>` : ''}</label>`).join('')}</div>`).join('')}
+    </div>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
+}
+
+function historySection() {
+  const history = data.colourHistory || [];
+  return `<section class="anc-page anc-history"><h2>${esc(t('anc.colourHistory', 'Colour changes'))}</h2>
+    <ul>${history.map((entry) => `<li><span class="anc-dot anc-${esc(entry.colour)}"></span> ${esc(new Date(entry.changedAt).toLocaleString())} · <strong>${esc(colourWord(entry.colour))}</strong> · ${esc(entry.changedBy)}
+      ${entry.reasons.length ? `<br><small>${entry.reasons.map((reason) => esc(reason.kh)).join(' · ')}</small>` : ''}</li>`).join('') || '<li>—</li>'}</ul></section>`;
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- tasks
+function taskButton(task) {
+  if (!canEdit() || task.state || task.autoDone) return '';
+  const action = task.action;
+  if (action.startsWith('order:')) {
+    const code = action.slice(6);
+    const allowed = code === 'ULTRASOUND' ? cfg.options.midwifeImaging.includes('ULTRASOUND') : cfg.options.midwifeLabTests.includes(code);
+    return allowed ? `<button type="button" class="link" data-order="${esc(code)}">${esc(code === 'ULTRASOUND' ? t('anc.orderUltrasound', 'Send to Imaging (pay at the Cashier)') : t('anc.orderLab', 'Send to the Laboratory (pay at the Cashier)'))}</button>`
+      : `<button type="button" class="link" data-ask-doctor="${esc(code)}">${esc(t('anc.askDoctor', 'Ask a doctor to order'))}</button>`;
+  }
+  if (action.startsWith('own:')) return `<button type="button" class="link" data-focus="${esc(action.slice(4))}">${esc(t('anc.enterResult', 'Enter the result'))}</button>`;
+  if (action === 'rx:iron') return `<button type="button" class="link" data-action="rx-iron">${esc(t('anc.addIron', 'Add to the prescription'))}</button>`;
+  if (action === 'vaccine:tt') return `<a class="link" href="/vaccination?patient=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}&vaccine=tt">${esc(t('anc.giveVaccine', 'Open the vaccination'))}</a>`;
+  return '';
+}
+function taskItem(task, overdue = false) {
+  const state = task.state;
+  const done = Boolean(state?.status === 'done' || task.autoDone);
+  const closed = state?.status === 'closed';
+  return `<li class="anc-task${done ? ' is-done' : ''}${closed ? ' is-closed' : ''}${overdue ? ' is-overdue' : ''}" data-task="${esc(task.key)}">
+    <label><input type="checkbox" data-task-done="${esc(task.key)}"${done ? ' checked' : ''}${canEdit() && !task.autoDone ? '' : ' disabled'}> ${esc(task.text)}</label>
+    ${closed ? `<small>${esc(state.reason)}${state.reasonText ? ` · ${esc(state.reasonText)}` : ''}</small>` : ''}
+    <span class="anc-task-actions">${taskButton(task)}${canEdit() && !done && !closed ? `<button type="button" class="link" data-task-close="${esc(task.key)}">${esc(t('anc.closeUndone', 'Close without doing'))}</button>` : ''}
+      ${canEdit() && (closed || state?.status === 'done') ? `<button type="button" class="link" data-task-reopen="${esc(task.key)}">${esc(t('anc.reopen', 'Reopen'))}</button>` : ''}</span></li>`;
+}
+function renderTasks(status) {
+  const host = $('anc-tasks');
+  if (!data.pregnancy) { host.innerHTML = ''; return; }
+  const header = status.scheduled ? `${t('anc.visitOf', 'Visit')} ${status.visit.visit}/${status.visitsPlanned} · ${status.visit.label}` : t('anc.notScheduled', 'Not a scheduled visit');
+  const next = status.next ? `${t('anc.nextVisit', 'Next scheduled visit')}: ${status.next.label} · ${status.appointment}` : '';
+  const stateOf = (task) => ({ ...task, state: record.tasks?.[task.key] || null });
+  const html = `<h2 id="anc-tasks-title">${esc(t('anc.todo', 'To do at this visit'))}</h2>
+    <p class="anc-task-visit">${esc(header)}</p>${next ? `<p class="anc-task-next">${esc(next)}</p>` : ''}
+    ${status.tasks.length ? `<ul class="anc-task-list">${status.tasks.map((task) => taskItem(stateOf(task))).join('')}</ul>` : ''}
+    ${status.overdue.length ? `<h3 class="anc-overdue-title">${esc(t('anc.overdue', 'Overdue from earlier visits'))}</h3><ul class="anc-task-list">${status.overdue.map((task) => taskItem(stateOf(task), true)).join('')}</ul>` : ''}`;
+  host.innerHTML = html;
+  if (!tasksShown && data.pregnancy?.status === 'open') {
+    tasksShown = true;
+    const dialog = $('anc-tasks-dialog');
+    dialog.innerHTML = `<div class="anc-dialog-body">${html}</div><div class="anc-actions"><button type="button" data-dialog-close>${esc(t('anc.ok', 'OK'))}</button></div>`;
+    dialog.showModal();
+  }
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- render all
+function renderMain() {
+  const main = $('anc-main');
+  if (!data.pregnancy) {
+    main.innerHTML = `<section class="anc-page"><p>${esc(t('anc.noRecord', 'No ANC record for this pregnancy yet.'))}</p>
+      ${data.canEdit ? `<button type="button" data-action="open">${esc(t('anc.open', 'Open the ANC record'))}</button>` : ''}</section>`;
+    return;
+  }
+  const status = currentStatus();
+  main.innerHTML = `${data.pregnancy.status === 'open' ? visitForm(status) : ''}${page2()}
+    <section class="anc-page" data-page="3">${labTable()}${ultrasoundTable()}${visitTable()}</section>${chartSection(status)}${historySection()}`;
+}
+function refreshLive() {
+  if (!data?.pregnancy) { renderTop(null); return; }
+  const status = currentStatus();
+  renderTop(status);
+  renderSummary(status);
+  renderTasks(status);
+  document.querySelectorAll('.anc-visit-name').forEach((node) => { node.textContent = status.scheduled ? `${t('anc.visitOf', 'Visit')} ${status.visit.visit}/${status.visitsPlanned} · ${status.visit.label}` : t('anc.notScheduled', 'Not a scheduled visit'); });
+}
+function renderAll() { renderMain(); refreshLive(); }
+
+// Today's visit starts from the saved visit of today, or from Triage.
+function startDraft() {
+  const saved = (data.visits || []).find((visit) => visit.date === data.today && visit.patientKey === patientKey);
+  const imported = data.imported || {};
+  draft = saved ? { ...saved } : { weight: imported.weightKg, bp: imported.bp, pulse: imported.pulse, temperature: imported.temperature, spo2: imported.spo2, rapid: {}, imported };
+  if (!draft.rapid) draft.rapid = {};
+  // Weight before pregnancy: from Triage at a first visit before 12 weeks.
+  const status = data.status;
+  if (record && !record.prePregnancyWeightKg && imported.weightKg && status?.ga && status.ga.weeks < (cfg.options.prePregnancyWeightFromTriageMaxWeeks || 12) && !(data.visits || []).length) {
+    record.prePregnancyWeightKg = imported.weightKg;
+    record.prePregnancyWeightSource = 'triage';
+  }
+  if (record && !record.heightCm && imported.heightCm) record.heightCm = imported.heightCm;
+  if (!draft.appointment && status?.appointment) draft.appointment = status.appointment;
+}
+
+async function load() {
+  try {
+    if (!cfg) cfg = await api('/api/maternal/config');
+    data = await api(`/api/anc/record?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}`);
+    record = data.pregnancy ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data.pregnancy.record || {})) : null;
+    if (record) startDraft();
+    renderAll();
+    setStatus('');
+  } catch (error) { setStatus(error.message, true); }
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- events
+document.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
+  const target = event.target;
+  if (target.dataset.path && record) {
+    setPath(record, target.dataset.path, target.type === 'checkbox' ? target.checked : target.value);
+    if (target.dataset.path === 'prePregnancyWeightKg') record.prePregnancyWeightSource = 'typed';
+    refreshLive();
+  } else if (target.dataset.visit) {
+    setPath(draft, target.dataset.visit, target.value);
+    refreshLive();
+  } else if (target.dataset.rx !== undefined) {
+    prescription[Number(target.dataset.rx)][target.dataset.rxKey] = target.value;
+  }
+});
+document.addEventListener('change', (event) => {
+  const target = event.target;
+  if (target.type === 'radio' && target.dataset.path && record) { setPath(record, target.dataset.path, target.value); refreshLive(); }
+  if (target.tagName === 'SELECT' && target.dataset.path && record) { setPath(record, target.dataset.path, target.value); refreshLive(); }
+  if (target.tagName === 'SELECT' && target.dataset.visit) { setPath(draft, target.dataset.visit, target.value); refreshLive(); }
+  if (target.tagName === 'SELECT' && target.dataset.rx !== undefined) { prescription[Number(target.dataset.rx)][target.dataset.rxKey] = target.value; renderMain(); }
+});
+
+const body = (extra = {}) => ({ patientKey, ...extra });
+async function run(action) {
+  setStatus('…');
+  try { await action(); setStatus(t('anc.saved', 'Saved.')); } catch (error) { setStatus(error.message, true); }
+}
+
+function addLine(item) {
+  if (!item) return;
+  const unit = window.BorkeoMedicine?.optionUnit?.(item) || item.unit;
+  prescription.push({ name: item.label, cmsCode: item.code || '', unit, route: 'po', doseAmount: '1', scheduleType: 'qd_m', d: '30', m: '', a: '', e: '', n: '', note: '' });
+  renderMain();
+}
+
+function closeDialog(event) {
+  const dialog = event.target.closest('dialog');
+  dialog?.close();
+}
+
+function askCloseReason(key) {
+  const dialog = $('anc-close-dialog');
+  const reasons = cfg.options.taskCloseReasons;
+  dialog.innerHTML = `<form method="dialog" class="anc-dialog-body"><h3>${esc(t('anc.closeUndone', 'Close without doing'))}</h3>
+    ${reasons.map((reason, index) => `<label class="anc-choice"><input type="radio" name="anc-close-reason" value="${esc(reason)}"${index === 0 ? ' checked' : ''}> ${esc(reason)}</label>`).join('<br>')}
+    <p><input type="text" class="anc-field wide" name="anc-close-text" maxlength="500" placeholder="${esc(t('anc.reason', 'Reason'))}"></p>
+    <p class="anc-error" role="status"></p>
+    <div class="anc-actions"><button type="button" class="secondary" data-dialog-close>${esc(t('drugSafety.cancel', 'Cancel'))}</button><button type="submit">${esc(t('drugSafety.save', 'Save'))}</button></div></form>`;
+  dialog.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    const reason = dialog.querySelector('input[name="anc-close-reason"]:checked')?.value || '';
+    const reasonText = dialog.querySelector('input[name="anc-close-text"]').value.trim();
+    try {
+      const result = await api(`/api/anc/record/${data.pregnancy.id}/task`, body({ key, action: 'close', reason, reasonText }));
+      record.tasks = result.pregnancy.record.tasks;
+      data.pregnancy = result.pregnancy;
+      dialog.close();
+      refreshLive();
+    } catch (error) { dialog.querySelector('.anc-error').textContent = error.message; }
+  });
+  dialog.showModal();
+}
+
+document.addEventListener('click', async (event) => {
+  const button = event.target.closest('button, a.link');
+  if (!button) return;
+  if (button.hasAttribute('data-dialog-close')) { closeDialog(event); return; }
+  const action = button.dataset.action;
+  const id = data?.pregnancy?.id;
+  if (action === 'open') return run(async () => { await api('/api/anc/record/open', body()); await load(); });
+  if (action === 'save-record') return run(async () => {
+    const result = await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/save`, body({ record, expectedVersion: data.pregnancy.version }));
+    data.pregnancy = result.pregnancy; data.colourHistory = result.colourHistory; data.status = result.status;
+    record = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(result.pregnancy.record)); renderAll();
+  });
+  if (action === 'save-visit') return run(async () => {
+    await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/save`, body({ record, expectedVersion: data.pregnancy.version }));
+    const status = currentStatus();
+    const visit = { ...draft, wp: weightGain(draft.weight), notScheduled: !status.scheduled };
+    delete visit.id;
+    await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/visit`, body({ visit }));
+    await load();
+  });
+  if (action === 'print') { printRecord(); return; }
+  if (action === 'print-referral') { printReferral(); return; }
+  if (action === 'referral') return run(async () => {
+    const treatment = window.prompt(t('anc.treatmentGiven', 'Treatment given before the referral')) ?? '';
+    await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/referral`, body({ treatment }));
+    await load();
+  });
+  if (action === 'sign') return run(async () => { await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/sign`, body()); await load(); });
+  if (action === 'close') return run(async () => {
+    const delivered = window.confirm(t('anc.closeDelivered', 'Has the woman delivered? OK = delivered, Cancel = close for another reason'));
+    const reason = delivered ? '' : (window.prompt(t('anc.reason', 'Reason')) || '');
+    if (!delivered && !reason) return;
+    await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/close`, body({ status: delivered ? 'delivered' : 'closed', reason }));
+    await load();
+  });
+  if (action === 'rx-add') { addLine((cfg.midwifeMedicines || []).find((item) => String(item.id) === $('anc-rx-pick').value)); return; }
+  if (action === 'rx-iron') {
+    const name = cfg.options.ironPrescriptionMedicine;
+    addLine((cfg.midwifeMedicines || []).find((item) => item.name === name && /tablet/i.test(item.form)) || (cfg.midwifeMedicines || []).find((item) => item.name === name));
+    document.getElementById('anc-visit-form')?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'end' });
+    return;
+  }
+  if (button.dataset.rxRemove !== undefined) { prescription.splice(Number(button.dataset.rxRemove), 1); renderMain(); return; }
+  if (action === 'rx-send') return run(async () => {
+    const medications = prescription.map((line) => ({ ...line, total: rxTotal(line) }));
+    await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/prescription`, body({ medications }));
+    const tablets = medications.filter((line) => /ferrous|folic/i.test(line.name)).reduce((sum, line) => sum + Number(line.total || 0), 0);
+    if (tablets) draft.fer = String(tablets);
+    prescription = [];
+    renderMain(); refreshLive();
+  });
+  if (button.dataset.order) return run(async () => {
+    const code = button.dataset.order;
+    const request = code === 'ULTRASOUND' ? { clinicalInfo: 'ANC', diagnosis: 'ANC' } : { items: [{ code }], clinicalInfo: 'ANC' };
+    await api('/api/paraclinic/orders', body({ modality: code === 'ULTRASOUND' ? 'ULTRASOUND' : 'LAB', request, chargeMode: 'PAID', handoffMode: 'DIRECT' }));
+    setStatus(t('anc.ordered', 'Sent. The patient pays at the Cashier.'));
+  });
+  if (button.dataset.askDoctor) return run(async () => { await api('/api/maternal/ask-doctor', body({ test: button.dataset.askDoctor })); });
+  if (button.dataset.focus) {
+    const map = { hiv: 'rapid.hiv', syphilis: 'rapid.syphilis', hb: 'rapid.hb', urine: 'proteinuria' };
+    const input = document.querySelector(`[data-visit="${map[button.dataset.focus]}"]`);
+    input?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' }); input?.focus();
+    $('anc-tasks-dialog').close?.();
+    return;
+  }
+  if (button.dataset.taskClose) { askCloseReason(button.dataset.taskClose); return; }
+  if (button.dataset.taskReopen) return run(async () => {
+    const result = await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/task`, body({ key: button.dataset.taskReopen, action: 'reopen' }));
+    record.tasks = result.pregnancy.record.tasks; refreshLive();
+  });
+});
+document.addEventListener('change', async (event) => {
+  const key = event.target.dataset.taskDone;
+  if (!key) return;
+  try {
+    const result = await api(`/api/anc/record/${data.pregnancy.id}/task`, body({ key, action: event.target.checked ? 'done' : 'reopen' }));
+    record.tasks = result.pregnancy.record.tasks;
+    refreshLive();
+  } catch (error) { setStatus(error.message, true); }
+});
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- print
+function printRecord() {
+  const status = currentStatus();
+  const header = `<header class="anc-print-head"><img src="/public/logo-hospital.jpg" alt=""><h1>${esc(L().title)}</h1>
+    <div class="anc-colour-boxes">${['green', 'yellow', 'red'].map((key) => `<span class="anc-colour-box anc-box-${key}"><span class="anc-box">${status.colour === key ? '✓' : ''}</span>${esc(colourWord(key))}</span>`).join('')}</div></header>`;
+  const footer = `<footer class="anc-print-foot">${esc(L().footer)}</footer>`;
+  const snapshot = $('anc-main').cloneNode(true);
+  snapshot.querySelectorAll('.anc-visit, .anc-chart, .anc-history, .anc-hint').forEach((node) => node.remove());
+  snapshot.querySelectorAll('input').forEach((input) => {
+    const span = document.createElement('span');
+    span.className = 'anc-print-value';
+    span.textContent = input.type === 'checkbox' || input.type === 'radio' ? (input.checked ? '☑' : '☐') : input.value;
+    input.replaceWith(span);
+  });
+  snapshot.querySelectorAll('select').forEach((select) => { const span = document.createElement('span'); span.textContent = select.selectedOptions[0]?.textContent || ''; select.replaceWith(span); });
+  const pages = Array.from(snapshot.querySelectorAll('.anc-page')).map((page) => `<div class="anc-print-page">${header}${page.innerHTML}${footer}</div>`).join('');
+  $('anc-print').innerHTML = pages;
+  document.body.classList.add('anc-printing');
+  window.addEventListener('afterprint', () => document.body.classList.remove('anc-printing'), { once: true });
+  window.print();
+}
+
+function printReferral() {
+  const visit = [...(data.visits || [])].reverse().find((item) => item.referral?.signed);
+  if (!visit) return;
+  const status = currentStatus();
+  $('anc-print').innerHTML = referralLetterHtml({ data, record, visit, status, labels: L(), t, esc });
+  document.body.classList.add('anc-printing', 'anc-printing-letter');
+  window.addEventListener('afterprint', () => document.body.classList.remove('anc-printing', 'anc-printing-letter'), { once: true });
+  window.print();
+}

+window.addEventListener('borkeo-language-change', () => data && renderAll());
+load();
